This is a property of the objective's optimum: the degenerate solution is a global minimum of the coupled predictor/EMA-target objective, present already at init. A repaired configuration reaches 14.377 of 14.379 bits, above the 13.865-bit oracle; reverting the loss to regression drops it to 0.307 bits, confirming it. Yet the repair licenses nothing about reasoning: the target is reducible, since intra-subgraph edges are a deterministic function of node census. The oracle reaches 96.4% of the ceiling, and our largest effect is the learning-rate schedule, not architecture. Bits and a reasoning probe show no relation across ten cells. A data-derived target fails a quality gate - 25.96% of nodes are duplicate placeholders, and the rest is more generic than supporting evidence. Rank, probes, and metrics can all saturate on an unsupportive evaluation. We release a harness with a reducibility audit and target gate.

</details>


### [168] [Learning Exact NVIDIA SASS Encoders with $\mathbb{F}_2$ Linear Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20532)
*Jiading Gai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: F2Asm是首个基于学习的NVIDIA SASS汇编器，能够精确重建128位SASS编码，支持Hopper、Blackwell和Rubin架构，通过在F2域上使用高斯消元法构建紧凑基底，实现对机器码重写的控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于NVIDIA未公开近期数据中心GPU的SASS汇编器，限制了对机器码的可控重写；为解决此问题，需要一个能从已知CUBIN反汇编结果中学习并重建正确编码的系统。

Method: F2Asm采用向量值仿射映射方法，在F2域上利用高斯消元法逐步构建编码空间的紧凑基底，分离控制位、重定位规则与元数据，并通过大量真实CUBIN样本进行训练。

Result: 在回环测试中，所有被反汇编后的SASS均能精确重新组装，且生成的可执行代码段与原始文件完全一致，验证了其准确性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: F2Asm是首个公开支持Rubin SM107的SASS汇编器，也是首个将SASS编码学习为F2域仿射映射的系统，为底层代码操控提供了新工具。

Abstract: NVIDIA provides a SASS disassembler but no public SASS assembler for recent data-center GPUs, limiting controlled machine-code rewriting. We present F2Asm, which learns exact 128-bit SASS encoders from paired disassembly and original CUBIN instruction words. To our knowledge, F2Asm is the first system to learn SASS instruction encoders as vector-valued affine maps over F2 and the first open-source NVIDIA SASS assembler to support Rubin SM107. F2Asm uses Gaussian elimination over F2 to incrementally build a compact basis, detect inconsistencies, and reject inputs outside the learned span. F2Asm separates target-specific control bits, relocation rules, and CUBIN metadata from its learning algorithm. We train encoders for Hopper SM90/SM90a, Blackwell SM100, and Rubin SM107 using 3,225 CUBINs from pinned NVIDIA and third-party production libraries, CUDA 13.3 packages, and CUDA 13.4 Developer Preview archives. In round-trip tests, F2Asm reassembles the disassembled SASS for each CUBIN, and all compared executable text sections match the originals exactly.

</details>


### [169] [AgentDecarbonizer: Carbon-Aware Execution for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20566)
*Leyi Yan,Shuangning Li,Sihang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了AI代理在执行长期任务时的碳排放问题，提出AgentDecarbonizer优化器，通过利用时间与空间上的碳强度差异来减少碳排放。该方法在考虑缓存重新计算开销和执行时间不确定性的情况下，保守估计任务持续时间并选择符合截止时间的低碳调度方案。实验表明，相比传统基线，碳排放降低最多达57.9%；相比仅在开始时选择最优电网的基线，也降低了37.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理在执行复杂任务时频繁调用大模型，导致显著的碳排放。现有方法未充分考虑碳排放的时间与空间变化特性，缺乏对执行延迟和缓存重载的合理处理机制，因此亟需一种能兼顾碳减排与任务时效性的优化策略。

Method: 提出AgentDecarbonizer，结合对任务执行时间的保守估计、碳强度动态感知、缓存重计算开销建模，以及基于用户设定截止时间的可行调度搜索，实现碳敏感型任务调度。

Result: 在WildClawBench上评估的60个任务中，AgentDecarbonizer相较于碳无关基线减少最多57.9%碳排放，相较于仅在任务开始时选择最优电网的基线减少最多37.5%碳排放，同时保证任务按时完成。

Conclusion: AgentDecarbonizer有效实现了AI代理任务的碳减排，证明了利用时间灵活性与地理迁移潜力是可行且高效的碳优化路径，为可持续智能系统设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: AI agents extend large language models from single prompt-response interactions to long-running, goaldirected workflows that issue many model calls, invoke tools, and interact with external environments. These workflows enable tasks such as software repair, data analysis, and experiment management, but their repeated model invocations can incur substantial carbon emissions. This paper characterizes the carbon emissions of OpenClaw agent workloads using WildClawBench, and shows that emissions depend on token consumption, context cache reuse, and the carbon intensity of the grid. Our characterization identifies deadline flexibility as an opportunity for carbon-aware execution: agent tasks can wait for lower-carbon-intensity periods or shift to lower-carbon grids. However, doing so requires handling uncertain execution time for temporal shifting and cached context recomputation during spatial shifting. We present AgentDecarbonizer, a carbon optimizer for AI agents that runs alongside OpenClaw. Given a task prompt and user-specified deadline, AgentDecarbonizer conservatively estimates task duration and selects deadline-feasible execution schedules, while accounting for cache recomputation overhead during spatial shifting. Evaluated on WildClawBench workloads with 60 agent tasks across four grids, AgentDecarbonizer reduces carbon emissions by up to 57.9 % compared with a carbon-agnostic baseline and by up to 37.5 % compared with a baseline that selects the carbon-optimal grid at task start time.

</details>


### [170] [Faults That Fortify: CNN Adversarial Robustness via GPU Undervolting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20572)
*Behnam Omidi,Ahmad Tahmasivand,Husam Alsyouri,Saba Al-Sayouri,Chongzhou Fang,Ihsen Alouani,Khaled N. Khasawneh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过GPU降压来同时解决卷积神经网络在对抗攻击下的脆弱性与训练成本过高的问题。降压引入的随机故障作为隐式正则化，提升了模型鲁棒性并显著降低能耗。实验表明，在标准和对抗训练下，降压模型均比正常电压模型具有更高的对抗准确率，且动态功耗随电压平方下降，实现能效与鲁棒性的协同提升。该方法无需算法修改，可直接部署于边缘设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNN在对抗攻击下易受攻击且训练成本高，尤其在资源受限的边缘设备上更为突出。现有对抗训练虽有效但代价高昂，亟需一种低成本、高效的方法同时提升模型鲁棒性和能效。

Method: 采用GPU降压技术，在训练过程中降低供电电压以引入硬件级随机扰动，利用这些扰动作为隐式正则化机制，从而增强模型对对抗样本的鲁棒性。通过分析位级故障特性，在LeNet、VGG-6和MobileNetV3上分别在MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上进行标准训练与对抗训练，对比不同电压条件下的性能表现。

Result: 在所有训练模式下，降压模型均表现出比正常电压模型更高的对抗准确率；同时，由于动态功耗与电压平方成正比，降压带来了显著的能耗降低。结果验证了硬件故障可增强模型鲁棒性，并实现能效与安全性的双赢。

Conclusion: GPU降压是一种无需算法改动的硬件级防御策略，能够有效提升CNN模型的对抗鲁棒性并大幅降低训练能耗，为边缘计算场景下的高效安全学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) face a dual challenge: vulnerability to adversarial attacks and prohibitive training cost. Adversarial training is effective but expensive, a burden that grows as learning shifts to the energy-constrained edge. This paper addresses both through GPU undervolting during training. Reducing supply voltage introduces stochastic perturbations that act as implicit regularization, improving robustness while lowering power. We characterize undervolting-induced faults at the bit level, then train LeNet, VGG-6, and MobileNetV3 on MNIST and CIFAR-10 under two training regimes, standard and adversarial, each at nominal and undervolted voltage, and evaluate all models against adversarial attacks. In both regimes, the undervolted model consistently achieves higher adversarial accuracy than its nominal-voltage counterpart, showing that hardware-induced faults strengthen even adversarial training. Because dynamic power scales quadratically with supply voltage, these robustness gains arrive with substantial energy savings. GPU undervolting is therefore a readily deployable hardware-level defense requiring no algorithmic change, and opens a promising direction in which robustness and energy efficiency move together.

</details>


### [171] [Provable Edge-of-Stability for Adam on a One-Dimensional Quadratic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20638)
*Yiman Fong,Heng Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过在一维二次函数上分析未经校正的Adam优化器，揭示了其边缘稳定性（EoS）现象的动态机制。在常曲率设定下，研究分离出优化器自身引起的动力学行为，发现Adam在多数参数区域中表现出向其冻结稳定性阈值 $2(1+β_1)/[η(1-β_1)]$ 恢复的趋势；同时识别出该机制失效的情形，如严格亚临界周期轨道和特殊调参轨迹。结果为Adam的EoS提供了清晰的动力学解释，并揭示其局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Adam优化器在边缘稳定性现象背后的动态机制，尤其是在损失曲面几何不变的理想设定下，以揭示其内在行为规律。

Method: 在单变量二次函数上分析未经校正的Adam优化器，利用常曲率环境隔离优化器本身的动态特性，通过理论推导与分析刻画不同参数区域下的动力学行为。

Result: 证明了在广泛参数范围内，Adam具有向稳定阈值 $2(1+β_1)/[η(1-β_1)]$ 恢复的倾向；同时发现某些特定条件下（如亚临界周期轨道或特殊收敛轨迹），该边缘探索机制会失效。

Conclusion: 该研究为Adam优化器的边缘稳定性现象提供了明确的动力学解释，强调其在理想条件下的有效性，同时也揭示了其适用范围的边界和潜在限制。

Abstract: The edge-of-stability (EoS) phenomenon of Adam has been widely observed, while its underlying dynamical mechanism is not yet fully understood. We study uncorrected Adam on a one-dimensional quadratic, a clean setting where constant curvature isolates the optimizer-induced dynamics behind the EoS. We characterize the resulting dynamics across the parameter space. In broad regimes, we prove that Adam exhibits a restoring tendency toward its frozen stability threshold $2(1+β_1)/[η(1-β_1)]$. We also identify settings in which this edge-seeking mechanism breaks down, including strictly subcritical periodic orbits and specially tuned trajectories that converge to the optimum while remaining uniformly supercritical. These results give a concrete dynamical explanation for Adam's EoS in a setting free of evolving loss geometry, while also exposing its limitations.

</details>


### [172] [Meta-clustering of milk mid-infrared spectra identifies dairy cow groups associated with negative energy balance in early lactation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20653)
*T. Touil,E. R. Paquet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用407,632个个体奶牛的中红外（MIR）光谱数据，结合多种光谱筛选、降维（PCA与自编码器）和聚类方法（k-means与谱聚类），共构建八种聚类策略。结果发现五种稳定的元聚类，显著关联于牛奶性状，且均反映早期泌乳期奶牛负能平衡（NEB）严重程度的梯度：重度、中度及可能轻度，另两个元聚类代表恢复阶段的奶牛，分别表现为快速恢复与早期恢复。尽管方法各异，八种策略均收敛至相同五元聚类，且经典的基于全谱的PCA-k-means方法可有效复现复杂方法的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接对中红外（MIR）光谱数据进行聚类有助于识别与牛奶性状或健康问题相关的奶牛群体，从而实现疾病预防与高风险个体监测。然而，如何有效从海量光谱数据中提取有意义的群组仍具挑战。

Method: 采用光谱过滤、主成分分析（PCA）、自编码器降维，结合k-means与谱聚类算法，构建八种聚类方法；通过合并相似聚类生成元聚类，以增强结果稳定性与可解释性。

Result: 识别出五个显著关联牛奶性状的元聚类，反映负能平衡（NEB）严重程度的梯度，包括重度、中度、轻度及两种恢复状态（快速与早期恢复）。八种方法高度一致，经典PCA-k-means方法表现良好，可替代复杂方法。

Conclusion: 基于MIR光谱的聚类分析可有效识别早期泌乳期奶牛的生物学群组，其结构与能量代谢状态密切相关。经典聚类方法在保持性能的同时具备更高的计算效率，适用于大规模应用。

Abstract: Clustering methods have been used to identify distinct groups of milk samples, cows, or herds. Fourier-transform infrared (FTIR) spectroscopy, particularly mid-infrared (MIR) spectroscopy, has been applied to individual cow milk samples to predict various milk traits. Applying clustering directly to MIR spectral data may reveal latent groups of cows associated with milk traits or health disorders and can help prevent these conditions or monitor at-risk animals. This study aimed to identify groups of individual dairy cows in early lactation directly from milk MIR spectra and to analyze their associations with milk traits. Using a dataset of 407,632 individual milk MIR records from 3,408 commercial farms, we combined (i) spectral filtering that selects informative wavenumbers, (ii) two dimensionality-reduction methods: principal component analysis (PCA) and an autoencoder, and (iii) two clustering algorithms: k-means and spectral clustering to yield eight different clustering approaches. We regrouped the assigned clusters into meta-clusters that encompassed the most similar ones identified by the eight approaches. Our results revealed five distinct meta-clusters of early-lactation individual dairy cows significantly associated with milk traits. Despite substantial differences, the eight approaches converged on the same five meta-clusters, and the classic, computationally efficient PCA-based k-means approach using the full spectrum recaptured clusters identified by more sophisticated, computationally intensive approaches. The five meta-clusters were strongly associated with DIM and appeared to reflect a gradient of negative energy balance (NEB) severity: severe, moderate, and possibly mild, while the remaining two likely represented cows recovering from NEB, one with rapid restoration of energy balance and one in early recovery.

</details>


### [173] [RiskTraf: Risk-Extrapolated Residual Learning for Multi-Variate Traffic Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20656)
*Guangyu Wang,Zhidan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PEMSB-3V基准和RiskTraf模型，利用原始流量、速度和占有率数据进行交通流预测，通过风险外推残差插件缓解不同交通状态下的偏差问题，显著提升多种骨干模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通流预测模型很少可靠地利用流量、速度和占有率三种原始测量值，且常因变量缺失、代理替换或逻辑不一致导致数据质量问题；此外，直接最小化经验风险可能在不同交通状态下产生依赖特定模式的捷径，影响模型泛化能力。

Method: 引入PEMSB-3V公共基准，保留原始三变量数据；提出模型无关的RiskTraf，冻结预训练骨干网络，基于历史速度与占有率学习轻量级零起点残差头，构建有序交通风险环境，并采用风险外推目标优化分时流量修正。

Result: 实验表明，RiskTraf能持续改进多种时空骨干模型，在多个指标上优于去偏和分布迁移适应方法，且无需修改原有模型结构。

Conclusion: PEMSB-3V与RiskTraf共同提供高质量数据支持与有效建模机制，显著提升交通流预测性能，尤其在复杂交通状态变化下表现优异。

Abstract: Traffic sensors commonly record flow, speed, and occupancy, but standard traffic flow forecasting benchmarks and models rarely exploit all three raw measurements reliably. Although speed and occupancy provide sensor-native traffic-state information beyond flow alone, existing releases often omit these variables, replace them with proxies, or contain logically inconsistent records. Moreover, direct empirical risk minimization over three-variable inputs may exploit regime-dependent shortcuts, as the relationships among flow, speed, and occupancy vary substantially between free-flow and congested states. We introduce \textbf{PEMSB-3V}, a public benchmark suite that preserves raw flow, speed, and occupancy measurements from PeMS detectors for flow prediction. We also propose \textbf{RiskTraf}, a model-agnostic risk-extrapolated residual plug-in. For each trained spatio-temporal backbone, RiskTraf freezes the selected checkpoint and learns a lightweight zero-start residual head from historical speed and occupancy. The residual head constructs ordered traffic-risk environments and optimizes horizon-wise flow corrections with a risk extrapolation objective, thereby mitigating regime-specific shortcut correlations without modifying the backbone. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RiskTraf consistently improves diverse forecasting backbones and outperforms debiasing and distribution-shift adaptation methods. Our code and benchmark are available at https://github.com/Guangyu4/RiskTraf.

</details>


### [174] [C-Score: Beyond Accuracy for Robustness Assessment in Semi-Supervised Learning under Open-World Unlabeled Contamination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20667)
*Tsao-Lun Chen,Chi-Cheng Fu,Han-Yi E. Chou,Shun-Feng Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在开放世界环境下，伪标签半监督学习（SSL）因外部数据污染导致的隐性退化问题。提出C-Score框架，从预测、特征表示和优化三个维度诊断训练行为，包含PLE、CCI、Sem-Drift和Grad-Align等指标。实验表明，即使干净分布上的准确率保持稳定，C-Score仍能检测到内部学习动态的显著恶化，证明仅依赖准确率无法可靠评估SSL在开放环境中的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有伪标签半监督学习方法通常假设未标记数据与标记数据来自相同分布（闭世界假设），但在实际应用中，未标记数据可能包含大量外部异常值（OOD）。这些异常值可能被高置信度预测并错误地加入训练，导致模型内部学习过程退化，但干净测试准确率却依然表现良好，从而掩盖了真实性能下降。因此需要一种能够揭示隐藏退化的评估机制。

Method: 提出C-Score框架，通过四个互补指标诊断伪标签SSL在开放世界下的训练行为：(1) PLE和CCI评估未标记样本的预测一致性；(2) Sem-Drift衡量特征表示相对于已知类别语义锚点的偏移；(3) Grad-Align分析标记与未标记样本间梯度方向的兼容性。该框架可全面捕捉模型在污染环境下的内在退化迹象。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上，使用SVHN作为污染源时，CCI指标上升超过280%，而最佳准确率仅下降3%以内；当使用近似OOD数据源（如CIFAR-100、STL-10）时，准确率最高下降达14.9%（以FlexMatch为例，污染比例r=0.5）。结果表明，仅靠干净准确率无法反映模型的真实退化状态，而C-Score能有效揭示隐藏的性能崩溃。

Conclusion: 清洁测试准确率在开放世界场景下不足以评估半监督学习模型的鲁棒性。必须结合内部诊断信号（如C-Score）才能实现对模型训练健康状况的可靠评估。未来工作应重视对模型内部行为的可观测性与可解释性设计。

Abstract: Pseudo-label-based semi-supervised learning has achieved strong performance due to its simplicity and scalability. However, it is typically developed under a closed-world assumption that unlabeled data are drawn from the same distribution as labeled data. In practical deployment, unlabeled data are often collected from open environments and may contain OOD samples. Under such contamination, OOD samples may still receive high-confidence predictions and be incorporated into training as if they were valid target examples. This creates an important evaluation problem: clean in-distribution test accuracy may appear stable even when the internal learning dynamics of SSL have already deteriorated. To address this issue, we study hidden collapse in pseudo-label-based SSL under open-world unlabeled contamination from a diagnostic evaluation perspective. We present C-Score, a compact framework that evaluates training behavior in three complementary spaces: prediction, feature representation, and optimization. C-Score includes PLE and CCI for unlabeled prediction behavior, Sem-Drift for deviation from labeled semantic anchors, and Grad-Align for the compatibility between labeled and unlabeled optimization. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 with multiple OOD sources, varying contamination ratios, and four pseudo-label-based SSL algorithms show that C-Score metrics reveal hidden degradation that clean accuracy alone fails to detect: under SVHN contamination, CCI rises over 280% while best-accuracy remains within 3% of the uncontaminated baseline; near-OOD sources (CIFAR-100, STL-10) cause up to 14.9% accuracy collapse (FlexMatch, r=0.5). The results suggest that clean accuracy alone is insufficient for evaluating SSL robustness in open-world environments, and that internal diagnostic signals are necessary for more reliable robustness assessment under unlabeled contamination.

</details>


### [175] [Lightweight Adaptive ReduNet via Hyperspherical Manifold Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20668)
*Zhenglin Huang,Qifa Yan,Bin Dai,Xiaohu Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LA-ReduNet 是一种轻量级自适应架构，通过改进层间更新规则，显著减少实现 MCR² 目标稳定所需的层数。该方法结合超球面流形学习与自适应步长，使参数存储量降低至原始 ReduNet 的约 1/29，同时保持相近的分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReduNet 虽具有数学可解释性，但其层间逐层构建过程需大量层数才能使 MCR² 目标稳定，导致参数存储开销大。为解决此问题，提出更高效的轻量化架构。

Method: 采用超球面流形学习与自适应步长机制，优化 ReduNet 的层间更新规则，提升收敛速度并减少所需层数。

Result: 实验表明，LA-ReduNet 在保持相近分类性能的前提下，使 MCR² 目标达到稳定状态所需的层数减少一个数量级，参数存储量仅为原 ReduNet 的约 1/29。

Conclusion: LA-ReduNet 有效提升了 ReduNet 的效率与可部署性，是实现高效、可解释神经网络的重要进展。

Abstract: In recent years, a white-box neural network called ReduNet has been proposed, which employs the maximal coding rate reduction (MCR$^2$) principle to transform raw data into low-dimensional discriminative features via a forward layer-wise construction process. Unlike traditional deep networks that rely on backpropagation, ReduNet explicitly derives the parameters of each layer from the features of its preceding layer, offering a mathematically interpretable paradigm. However, this layer-wise construction often requires a large number of layers for the MCR$^2$ objective to reach a stable value, which increases the parameter storage of the unfolded module. To address this issue, we propose LA-ReduNet, a lightweight adaptive architecture that refines the layer-wise update rule and enables discriminative feature representations to be obtained with substantially fewer unfolded layers. Specifically, LA-ReduNet employs hyperspherical manifold learning and adaptive step sizes, thereby reducing by an order of magnitude the number of layers required for the MCR$^2$ objective to reach a stable value. Simulation results demonstrate that, while maintaining comparable classification accuracy, LA-ReduNet requires significantly fewer layers for the MCR$^2$ objective to reach a stable value. Remarkably, under the considered experimental settings, LA-ReduNet requires only approximately $1/29$ of the parameter storage of the unfolded ReduNet module for the MCR$^2$ objective to reach a stable value.

</details>


### [176] [Reinforcement Learning for Continuous-Time Jump Markov Decision Processes with Applications to Network Dynamic Pricing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20680)
*Huiling Meng,Ningyuan Chen,Xuefeng Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究连续时间跳跃马尔可夫决策过程（CTJMDPs）中的强化学习，针对一般离散状态空间和连续/离散动作空间，提出基于熵正则化的连续时间控制框架，并建立适用于此类系统的Q-learning理论基础与模型无关算法。相比传统的时间离散化方法，该方法在理论和实证上均具优势，在网络动态定价等大规模场景中表现出更优的策略学习能力与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续时间强化学习方法主要针对欧几里得空间中的连续状态，依赖半鞅理论，难以应用于缺乏向量空间结构的一般离散状态空间的CTJMDPs。因此需要建立适用于此类问题的理论框架与算法。

Method: 提出熵正则化的连续时间控制模型，构建适用于一般离散状态空间的Q-learning理论基础，设计模型无关的Q-learning算法，避免对系统动态进行显式建模。

Result: 所提算法在多产品动态定价等网络场景中能稳定学习近似最优策略，显著优于标准基准方法，具备良好的解质量与大规模可扩展性。

Conclusion: 本文为连续时间跳跃马尔可夫决策过程建立了坚实的强化学习理论基础，提出的模型无关Q-learning算法有效克服了传统方法在离散状态空间中的局限性，展现出优越的性能与实用性。

Abstract: We study reinforcement learning (RL) in Continuous-Time Jump Markov Decision Processes (CTJMDPs) featuring general discrete state spaces (which need not possess a vector space structure) and continuous/discrete action spaces. The setup covers many well-known applications in operations such as multi-product dynamic pricing with capacitated resources (Gallego and van Ryzin 1997). To model the exploration-exploitation tradeoff, we formulate an entropy-regularized continuous-time control problem with stochastic policies. Recent continuous-time RL techniques such as $q$-learning for controlled diffusions in (Jia and Zhou 2023) focus on continuous state spaces $\mathbb{R}^d$ and rely heavily on semimartingale theory in $\mathbb{R}^d$ for their theoretical analysis. Consequently, their methods cannot be directly applied to CTJMDPs with general discrete state spaces, which may lack the algebraic addition and subtraction structures inherent to Euclidean spaces. To bridge this gap, we establish the theoretical foundations of $q$-learning for CTJMDPs and develop model-free $q$-learning algorithms. Compared to naïve time discretization and approximating CTJMDPs using discrete-time MDPs, our approach has several conceptual and empirical benefits. Numerical experiments in network dynamic pricing (Gallego and van Ryzin 1997) show that our proposed RL algorithm reliably learns near-optimal policies and consistently outperforms standard benchmark methods, demonstrating superior solution quality and effective scalability to large-scale network instances.

</details>


### [177] [Geometric Regularization for Long-Tailed Semi-Supervised Learning via Gaussian Feature Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20710)
*Hongyang He,Xinyuan Song,Yan Zhong,Daizong Liu,Yanbin Li,Yang-fan He,Wenqiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Gaussian Bridge Consistency (GBC)框架，通过构建类条件高斯特征桥实现无标签样本与高质量类别原型间的语义插值，结合动态原型图谱和桥一致性损失，提升长尾半监督学习中模型的泛化能力与抗噪声性能。引入BridgeMix策略增强跨样本泛化，实验验证在CIFAR10-LT和ImageNet-LT上显著改善长尾类别表现，兼具有效性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界半监督学习面临长尾标签分布和伪标签噪声问题，导致模型泛化能力下降并加剧确认偏差。现有方法难以有效处理此类挑战，亟需更鲁棒的机制来提升长尾类别性能。

Method: 设计GBC框架，利用动态原型图谱存储每类的标注与伪标注样例；为每个无标签样本构建类条件高斯特征桥，在潜在空间中实现从不确定预测到可靠原型的平滑轨迹；引入桥一致性损失，强制模型沿插值路径对齐几何目标分布；提出BridgeMix策略，基于置信度进行样本与原型对的特征混合，增强泛化能力。

Result: 在CIFAR10-LT和ImageNet-LT（USB基准）上的大量实验表明，GBC在长尾半监督设置下表现出强鲁棒性和有效性，显著提升长尾类别性能，且不牺牲模型可扩展性。

Conclusion: GBC通过语义插值与一致性约束，有效缓解了长尾分布与伪标签噪声带来的负面影响，为现实场景下的半监督学习提供了高效、稳健的新范式。

Abstract: Real-world semi-supervised learning (SSL) often encounters significant challenges with long-tailed label distributions and noisy pseudo-labels, which hinder generalization and amplify confirmation bias. In this work, we introduce a novel framework, Gaussian Bridge Consistency (GBC), to address these challenges by constructing semantic interpolation paths between unlabeled samples and high-quality class anchors. Our method maintains a dynamic Prototype Atlas that stores a diverse and evolving set of labeled and pseudo-labeled exemplars per class. For each unlabeled instance, GBC forms a class-conditional Gaussian Feature Bridge in the latent space, enabling the student model to traverse a smooth trajectory from uncertain predictions to reliable class prototypes. A bridge consistency loss is applied along this path to enforce alignment with a geometrically interpolated target distribution. Furthermore, we propose BridgeMix, a confidence-aware feature mixing strategy that interpolates both sample and anchor pairs to amplify cross-sample generalization. Extensive experiments on CIFAR10-LT and ImageNet-LT (USB benchmarks) validate the robustness and effectiveness of GBC under realistic long-tailed SSL settings, consistently improving long tail-class performance without sacrificing scalability.

</details>


### [178] [Hidden Axis of Uncertainty: Latent-Posterior Alignment in Graph Neural Networks with Bayesian Output Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20758)
*Suk Hoon Choi,Damdae Park,Junhyuk Choi,Hyein Jung,Changsoo Kim,Ung Lee,Kyeongsu Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了图神经网络（GNN）中贝叶斯输出层的预测不确定性机制。传统理论认为不确定性降低源于后验收缩，但在深度模型中该假设未必成立。研究发现，即使后验方差未收缩，预测不确定性仍会因潜在表示向低方差后验方向移动而减少，这种现象称为潜在-后验对齐（LPA）。通过干预实验验证了LPA的功能作用，并提出一种名为对齐引导学习（AGL）的新方法，显式促进该对齐过程。AGL在保持精度的同时有效降低不确定性，改善结构校准，使模型置信度更准确反映数据密度分布。研究揭示了GNN中不确定性动态的新视角，强调潜在空间与参数空间之间的几何关系，而非单纯依赖后验幅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解贝叶斯图神经网络中预测不确定性的形成机制，突破传统后验收缩理论的局限，揭示深层模型中不确定性变化的真实驱动因素。

Method: 通过观察和干预实验分析潜在表示与后验方向之间的几何关系，提出并验证‘潜在-后验对齐’（LPA）机制，并设计‘对齐引导学习’（AGL）方法以主动优化该对齐过程。

Result: AGL方法在不牺牲模型精度的前提下显著降低预测不确定性，提升结构校准性能；实验证明潜在表示向低方差后验方向移动是不确定性下降的关键因素，而非后验收缩。

Conclusion: 预测不确定性在贝叶斯GNN中的变化主要由潜在表示与后验方向之间的几何对齐驱动，而非后验方差的收缩。这一发现为理解深度模型中的不确定性提供了新范式，并通过AGL实现了更可靠、可解释的不确定性建模。

Abstract: Bayesian Neural Networks (BNNs) with Bayesian output layers provide a principled and tractable framework for quantifying predictive uncertainty, yet the mechanisms shaping that uncertainty remain unclear. While conventional theory attributes uncertainty reduction to posterior contraction, the corresponding assumptions need not hold for deep models. In the Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) with Bayesian output layers studied here, we observe that predictive uncertainty decreases as latent representations shift toward lower-variance posterior directions, even though the posterior variance does not contract. We term this behavior Latent-Posterior Alignment (LPA) and conduct interventional experiments that support its functional role in shaping predictive uncertainty. Building on this insight, we propose Alignment-Guided Learning (AGL), which explicitly promotes this alignment during training. AGL effectively reduces predictive uncertainty while preserving accuracy and improves structural calibration, ensuring that the model confidence faithfully mirrors underlying data density. These findings provide a new perspective on uncertainty dynamics in GNNs with mean-field Bayesian output layers, shifting the focus from the magnitude of the posterior to the geometric interplay between latent and parameter spaces.

</details>


### [179] [Fuzzy-MoE: Interpretable Regime-Conditioned Expert Routing for Non-Stationary Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20761)
*Lan Guo,Jie Xiao,Zhao Su,Jun Shen,Haoran Li,Weixia Ma,Qingguo Zhou,Binbin Yong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fuzzy-MoE 提出一种基于模糊逻辑的动态混合专家模型，用于非平稳多变量时间序列预测。通过双视角模糊路由器结合局部卷积动态与全局分段统计，实现对潜在时序状态的识别，并利用可学习的高斯隶属函数计算专家激活强度，支持细粒度的专家路由。该方法允许同一序列中不同变量激活不同专家，有效捕捉异质性时序动态，提升模型可解释性。实验表明，Fuzzy-MoE 在多个公开数据集上显著优于主流方法，且其模糊隶属度和规则激活提供了透明的路由诊断，实现了预测性能与机制透明性的双重提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度预测模型通常将异质的潜在动态压缩为统一的端到端映射，导致对时变动态建模不足且缺乏可解释性，难以区分在不同潜在状态下激活的预测机制。

Method: 提出 Fuzzy-MoE 模型，包含多个并行专家网络和双视角模糊路由器；路由器通过联合利用局部卷积动态与全局分段统计，借助可学习的高斯隶属函数推断潜在时序状态，并生成基于 IF-THEN 规则的专家激活强度，实现可解释的专家路由。

Result: 在多个公开时间序列基准数据集上，Fuzzy-MoE 显著优于主流预测方法，在预测精度上表现更优；同时，模糊隶属度与规则激活提供了可解释的路由诊断，验证了框架在预测性能与机制透明性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: Fuzzy-MoE 通过引入基于模糊逻辑的可解释专家路由机制，有效解决了传统 MoE 模型黑箱路由的问题，实现了对异质时序动态的精细建模与机制透明化，是提升时间序列预测模型性能与可解释性的有效方案。

Abstract: In non-stationary multivariate time series, different variables and samples often exhibit heterogeneous latent dynamic states, while existing deep forecasting models usually compress them into a unified end-to-end mapping, leading to suboptimal modeling of time-varying dynamics and limited interpretability regarding which forecasting mechanism is activated under different latent states. To overcome these limitations, we reformulate time series forecasting as a unified framework of latent temporal state identification and interpretable expert routing, and propose Fuzzy-MoE, a fuzzy logic-based dynamic Mixture-of-Experts model. Fuzzy-MoE consists of multiple parallel expert mapping networks and a dual-view fuzzy router. By jointly exploiting local convolutional dynamics and global segmented statistics, the router infers latent temporal states and computes expert activation strengths through learnable Gaussian membership functions, enabling explicit IF-THEN rule-based expert selection. This fine-grained routing strategy allows different variables within the same sequence to activate different experts, effectively capturing heterogeneous temporal dynamics while improving model interpretability. Experimental results on multiple public time series benchmark datasets show that Fuzzy-MoE significantly outperforms mainstream forecasting methods in forecasting accuracy. Moreover, fuzzy memberships and rule activations provide interpretable routing diagnostics, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed framework in both forecasting performance and mechanism transparency. Unlike traditional MoE models that use black-box routing, Fuzzy-MoE`s routing is based on clear, interpretable fuzzy rules. This makes the expert selection transparent and traceable.

</details>


### [180] [Resolution-Consistent Greedy Neural Approximation on Infinite-Dimensional Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20812)
*Pablo M. Berná,Antonio Falcó,Diego Mondéjar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在有限坐标观测下具有无限维输入的浅层神经网络模型的构造性近似与学习保证。基于参数归一化的神经词典及其相关加权变差类，近似误差被分解为依赖分布的坐标截断项和贪婪有限宽度项。对于经验回归，全修正贪婪算法提供了对总体的保证，其统计复杂度在保留的输入分辨率上是统一的。该框架还可扩展到希尔伯特空间值响应，且不显式依赖输出维度。这些无维度陈述是统计性的，而非计算性的：选择新神经元仍需解决非凸参数搜索问题。以最近无限维通用逼近结果背后的准波兰构造为例，并通过合成实验验证了预测的分辨率、宽度和样本量范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无限维输入神经网络在有限观测坐标下的近似与学习性能，旨在建立理论保证并理解不同因素（如分辨率、宽度、样本量）对模型表现的影响。

Method: 采用参数归一化的神经词典和加权变差类分析框架，将近似误差分解为坐标截断与贪婪宽度两部分；使用全修正贪婪算法进行经验回归，获得统一于输入分辨率的统计保证；推广至希尔伯特值响应场景。

Result: 建立了针对无限维输入神经网络的构造性近似与学习保证，揭示了近似误差的组成结构；提出了在不同输入分辨率下具有统一统计复杂度的算法；实验验证了理论预测的分辨率、宽度与样本量关系。

Conclusion: 本工作为无限维输入神经网络提供了理论支撑，展示了在有限观测下实现良好泛化能力的可能性，同时强调了统计性质与计算挑战之间的张力。

Abstract: We develop constructive approximation and learning guarantees for shallow neural models with infinite-dimensional inputs observed through finitely many coordinates. The analysis is based on a parameter-normalized neural dictionary and its associated weighted variation class. Within this class, the approximation error separates into a distribution-dependent coordinate-truncation term and a greedy finite-width term. For empirical regression, a fully-corrective greedy procedure yields population guarantees whose statistical complexity is uniform in the retained input resolution. The same framework extends to Hilbert-valued responses without an explicit dependence on the output dimension. The dimension-free statements are statistical, not computational: selecting a new neuron still requires solving a nonconvex parameter-search problem. The quasi-Polish construction underlying recent infinite-dimensional universal approximation results provides a motivating example, and synthetic experiments illustrate the predicted resolution, width, and sample-size regimes.

</details>


### [181] [Scaling Muon for Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20818)
*Chenghao Li,Xiao Han,Xinxin Huang,Wei Liu,Boyang Li,Bing Xiao,Heran Zhang,Juanma Perez Rua,Ke Xu,Kangning Liu,Linjun Kuang,Na Li,Tan Wang,Tian Xie,Wei Peng,Yang Pei,Yifan Xu,Yuanhao Zhai,Yuwei Lin,Zhe Wang,Zihao He,Daniel Li,Junbiao Tang,Ziyang Jiang,Dake Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Muon优化器在大模型训练中通过平衡奇异方向更新提升性能，但其在大规模扩散变换器（DiTs）上的扩展性和端到端效率尚不明确。本文研究了从1.3B到15B参数规模的Muon扩展行为，发现其在优化和生成质量上持续优于AdamW。然而，在大规模下，每步执行5步Newton-Schulz迭代（NS5）及全动量显式存储带来显著计算与通信开销，抵消了其步骤效率优势。为此提出周期性行级Muon（Periodic Row-wise Muon），仅每K步进行一次完整的NS5谱更新，其余步骤采用低开销的行级约束更新。同时设计分布式实现，非刷新步直接在分片动量上操作，并通过桶化all-gather和通信-计算重叠加速谱刷新。实验表明，该方法在所有规模下均比AdamW提升生成质量12.9–19.1%；相比原Muon，在1.3B–4B模型上保持<0.5%差距，9B模型提升4.5%；优化时间减少46.9–54.3%，端到端步时间减少15.7–24.3%，逻辑通信量降低66.7%，达到最佳生成质量所需活跃训练时间减少33.7–64.8%。结果表明，周期性行级Muon在保留生成质量优势的同时，显著提升了大规模DiTs的端到端训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有矩阵感知优化器Muon虽能提升大模型训练性能，但在大规模扩散变换器（DiTs）上存在计算与通信开销过大的问题，导致其端到端效率受限，亟需在保持生成质量优势的前提下提升实际训练效率。

Method: 提出周期性行级Muon：每K步执行一次完整的5步Newton-Schulz迭代（NS5）进行谱更新，其余步骤采用低开销的行级约束动量更新；设计分布式实现，支持分片动量上的直接操作，并通过桶化all-gather和通信-计算重叠加速谱刷新过程。

Result: 在1.3B至15B参数规模的DiTs上，周期性行级Muon相比AdamW提升生成质量12.9–19.1%；相比原Muon，在1.3B–4B模型上生成质量损失<0.5%，9B模型提升4.5%；优化时间减少46.9–54.3%，端到端步时间减少15.7–24.3%，逻辑通信量降低66.7%，达到最佳生成质量所需活跃训练时间减少33.7–64.8%。

Conclusion: 周期性行级Muon在保持Muon生成质量优势的基础上，显著降低了计算、通信和训练时间开销，成功将矩阵感知优化器的优势转化为大规模扩散变换器的实际端到端训练效率提升。

Abstract: The matrix-aware optimizer Muon improves large model training by balancing updates across singular directions, yet its scaling behavior and end-to-end efficiency on large Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) remain unclear. We first establish Muon's scaling behavior on DiTs from 1.3B to 15B parameters, showing that its optimization and generative quality advantages over AdamW persist across model scales. However, at scale, the 5-step Newton--Schulz iteration (NS5) performed at every optimization step, together with full-momentum materialization, introduces substantial computation and communication overhead that can offset Muon's step-efficiency advantage. We introduce \emph{Periodic Row-wise Muon}, which performs a full NS5 spectral update once every \(K\) steps and applies a low compute and communication cost row-wise constrained update based on the current momentum at the remaining steps. We further co-design a distributed implementation that operates directly on sharded momentum during non-refresh steps and accelerates spectral refreshes through bucketed all-gather and communication--computation overlap. Across all scales, Muon improves the best observed generative quality over AdamW by 12.9--19.1\%. Compared with vanilla Muon, Periodic Row-wise Muon remains within 0.5\% in best generative quality on the 1.3B--4B models and improves it by 4.5\% at 9B. It reduces optimizer time by 46.9--54.3\%, end-to-end step time by 15.7--24.3\%, and logical communication volume by 66.7\%, while reaching its respective best generative quality with 33.7--64.8\% less active training time. These results show that Periodic Row-wise Muon preserves Muon's generative quality advantage while translating it into end-to-end training efficiency for large DiTs.

</details>


### [182] [Nothing Changed but the Model: CellFill -- Bounded In-Cell Learning for Bit-Identical, Revocable Updates to Quantized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20873)
*Zifeng Liu,Zhiyong Du,Yaxin Lu,Yiming Mao,Zhenhe Wang,Wenqi Shi,Zhengkun Jing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在量化间隙中学习新知识的方法，通过冻结4位释放模型的整数码和缩放因子，将新知识仅写入每个量化决策单元内的权重残差，实现可逆、可验证且漂移受限的更新。该方法在不改变原始检查点的前提下，保持了比特级一致性，支持精确不变性，并在多个任务上表现出优于传统方法的跨域遗忘控制与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型更新方法（如全微调、适配器合并、模型编辑）均需替换发布检查点，导致所有依赖该检查点的评估与缓存失效；本文旨在避免这一问题，实现无需修改原始模型、可撤销、可验证的知识更新。

Method: 在量化间隙中引入权重残差，冻结4位模型的整数码与缩放参数，仅更新每权重残差部分，再通过重新量化恢复原始比特表示，保证输出完全一致；提出CellFill等三种训练路径，实现结构化不变性。

Result: 在三个种子下，受约束的密集路径与无约束参考模型在事实召回率上接近（58.9% vs 59.3%），跨域困惑度更优；投影到量化细胞显著减少跨域遗忘，且在收敛情况下表现一致；不同方法在相同重演条件下，以更低的跨域困惑度代价获取知识，效率排名与召回率不一致；该方法成功迁移至270亿参数混合线性注意力模型并保持比特级一致。

Conclusion: 精确不变性几乎免费实现，该方法提供了一种高效、可逆、可验证的模型更新机制，突破了传统方法的限制，在保持原始模型完整性的同时有效提升知识注入效率。

Abstract: Every way of teaching a deployed language model something new -- full fine-tuning, adapter merging, model editing -- replaces the released checkpoint, and with it every evaluation and cache that referred to those exact bits. We instead learn inside the dequantization gap: with the integer codes and scales of a 4-bit release frozen, new knowledge is written only into the per-weight residual that lives strictly inside each quantization decision cell. Re-quantization then returns the released artifact bit-for-bit, a machine-checkable guarantee; updates are exactly revocable by dropping the residual; and drift is bounded. We give six propositions and three training paths, including CellFill, a bounded reparameterization that makes invariance structural rather than enforced.
  Exact invariance turns out to be nearly free: across three paired seeds the constrained dense path matches an unconstrained reference whose weights provably escape the artifact (58.9 vs 59.3 percent fact recall; paired difference -0.5 points, 95% CI [-5.0,+4.0]), and is better on held-out cross-domain perplexity. Against the natural null hypothesis -- serving the same update as an unmerged adapter -- projecting into the cells reduces cross-domain forgetting in every run that converged, and a diverged control shows the boundary: projection is a trust region, not a repair. What no method escapes is the cost of knowledge itself, and the apparent free lunch of in-domain perplexity improving past the anchor is an artifact of rehearsal sharing a corpus with the metric. Methods differ threefold at matched rehearsal in knowledge bought per point of cross-domain perplexity, a ranking that is not the recall ranking. The method transfers to a 27B hybrid linear-attention model (2.4e10 constrained weights, verified bit-identical), where matched recall costs about half as much cross-domain perplexity as at 1.7B.

</details>


### [183] [Decoupling Policy Extraction for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20909)
*Xuyao Lin,Yixiang Shan,Jinru Duan,Tao Yang,Xinyu Zhao,Runyu Lei,Yiming Zhao,Jiaxin Fan,Zongbao Feng,Peng Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种解耦的策略提取范式，将策略改进与演员训练分离。在该范式下，演员仅用于建模行为分布，而价值评估由独立训练的评论家在推理时对多个演员生成的候选动作进行重排序。这种方法避免了传统联合训练中因固定数据导致的过估计和分布外动作漂移问题，在多种实验中优于行为克隆和联合学习方法，且即使使用简单的Q-learning评论家也表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离线强化学习中，演员与评论家联合训练，但在固定数据环境下，演员改进无法产生新数据来验证或修正评论家，导致策略更新可能偏离数据支持区域，引发过估计和分布外动作问题；同时，保守价值估计与高价值动作选择之间存在难以调和的权衡。

Method: 提出解耦策略提取范式：演员仅负责建模行为分布，生成一系列符合行为分布的动作候选；在推理阶段，使用独立训练的评论家对这些候选动作进行价值评估并重排序，实现策略改进。

Result: 实验表明，该范式在多种任务上显著优于行为克隆和联合训练方法，即使使用基础的Q-learning评论家也能保持良好性能，展现出更强的鲁棒性和有效性。

Conclusion: 解耦策略提取范式有效缓解了离线强化学习中的过估计和分布外动作问题，通过分离行为建模与价值选择，提升了策略质量与稳定性，为离线强化学习提供了新的有效框架。

Abstract: Offline RL methods commonly jointly train the actor and critic, where the critic is used to guide the actor toward higher-value actions. This coupled learning process is well motivated in online RL, where an improved actor collects new data that can further update the actor and the critic. However, training data remains fixed in offline RL, making actor-side policy improvement unable to generate new data to validate or correct the critic. Moreover, retaining this coupled paradigm leads to two related challenges. Firstly, actor updates can drift toward high-valued but potentially out-of-distribution (OOD) actions and amplify critic overestimation. Secondly, conservative value estimation or behavior-cloning regularization creates a difficult trade-off between suppressing OOD actions and selecting high-value actions within the data-supported region. Motivated by this observation, we revisit the conventional offline RL paradigm and propose decoupling policy improvement from actor training. Specifically, we train the actor solely to model the behavior distribution and perform policy improvement at inference time by reranking multiple actor-generated proposals with a separately learned critic. We refer to this paradigm as the decoupled policy extraction paradigm. Under such paradigm, the actor provides behavior-supported action candidates, while the critic performs value-based selection within this candidate set. Extensive experiments show that the decoupled policy extraction paradigm outperforms both behavior cloning and jointly learned offline RL methods, while remaining effective even with a naive Q-learning critic.

</details>


### [184] [Training, learning and inference: unified dynamics of neural systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20965)
*Mian Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种原子生成事实模型，构建生成事实图（GFG）以记录生成过程的历史，并建立基于GFG的递归科学流程。通过nanoGPT实现统一的训练-学习动态，揭示参数优化系统的状态演化与功能重组机制。提出三重坐标预测框架，实现高精度预测（91.43%准确率，91.49%宏平均召回率）。进一步将推理定义为训练-学习动态的冻结投影，验证了组件门控、回滚及注意力机制中的因果支持作用，且在多种模型和任务中证实了接收状态依赖响应与持续支持重组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统缺乏对生成过程历史的可追溯性与可编译性科学事实基础，难以支持分析、干预、重播与验证的闭环科学流程。本文旨在构建一种可追踪、可计算的生成事实表示体系，以支撑可解释、可控制的智能系统演化。

Method: 定义原子生成事实f=(u,tau,omega,z;rho)，构建生成事实图（GFG）；建立基于GFG的递归科学过程；利用nanoGPT实现统一训练-学习动态建模；引入三重坐标（目标边界状态、目标特定更新几何、参数-Adam接收状态）构建二阶预测器；通过组件门控、回滚、受控反馈等机制验证因果支持与非加性组合；将推理视为训练-学习动态的冻结投影。

Result: 在保持测试集上达到91.43%准确率与91.49%宏平均召回率；验证了接收状态条件下的响应差异、持续支持重组及推理的冻结投影特性；多模型实验（ResNet/CIFAR-100、扩散模型/CIFAR-10）确认方法泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本研究提出了一种可编译、可追溯的生成事实框架（GFG），实现了对训练-学习动态的深层建模与可解释性分析，为构建具有自省能力的下一代人工智能系统提供了理论与技术基础。

Abstract: We define an atomic generation fact f=(u,tau,omega,z;rho), recording the origin, realized transformation, concrete occurrence, generated result and relation role. Compiled into a Generation-Fact Graph (GFG), these facts provide an AI-native, compilable scientific fact substrate preserving generation histories. We establish a GFG-based recursive scientific process in which analysis, intervention, replay and validation form facts for later cycles. Using nanoGPT, we establish unified training-learning dynamics. Training is the evolution of a parameter-optimizer system with state and memory: each actual training action enters the receiving state and produces a finite-amplitude nonlinear functional response conditioned by that state and target-specific update geometry. Learning is the persistent reorganization of distributed functional support by these responses; capability formation, maintenance, decline or recovery becomes observable when target-specific states are evaluated against their readout boundaries. Three primary coordinates - target-boundary state, target-specific update geometry and parameter-Adam receiving state - yield a second-order predictor operating before post-update outputs are read. On held-out runs, it achieved 91.43% accuracy and 91.49% macro-averaged recall across four transitions. We further establish inference as a frozen projection of training-learning dynamics. Component gating and rollback show causal recruitment and non-additive combination of query-conditioned support formed during training, deriving organizational conditions realized by Attention. Controlled feedback indicates possible double-edged reinforcement effects. ResNet/CIFAR-100 and diffusion/CIFAR-10 experiments confirm receiving-state-conditioned responses, persistent support reorganization and frozen inference projection beyond nanoGPT.

</details>


### [185] [A Critical Audit of Spatiotemporal Forecasting Benchmark Datasets and Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20980)
*Kenneth Martin,Simon Heilig,Asja Fischer,Michel F. C. Haddad,Adam M. Sykulski,Moshe Eliasof*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了广泛使用的基准数据集在多变量时间序列短时预测中的局限性，揭示了空间无关线性模型表现强劲的原因，并指出一阶差分数据引入的结构偏差。研究建议减少对这些数据集的依赖，提倡更严格的统计评估方法，并通过简单混合模型验证了新方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN方法在多变量时间序列预测中主要基于少数几个基准数据集进行评估，但这些数据集的评价协议中线性模型表现接近甚至优于GNN，引发对其判别能力的质疑。因此需要深入分析数据集本身，以理解性能差异的根源。

Method: 采用经典时间序列分析方法对基准数据集进行统计分析，识别显著的时间与空间相关性，检测一阶差分带来的结构偏差，并提出改进评估框架的建议。

Result: 发现一阶差分处理导致数据中隐藏的空间-时间结构偏差，使得不考虑空间信息的线性模型仍能取得良好表现；这削弱了现有基准数据集对GNN模型的区分能力。

Conclusion: 应减少对当前主流基准数据集的依赖，推动更严谨的统计评估机制；本研究提出的方法可指导未来GNN模型设计，提升其实际有效性与可解释性。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are routinely employed for short-range forecasting on multivariate time series with a spatial graph structure. Despite the availability of many alternative datasets, method innovations within this domain are predominantly assessed against a rather limited set of benchmark datasets, most notably Chickenpox, PedalMe, WikiMaths, METR-LA, and PEMS-BAY. The evaluation protocols contain baselines spanning from historical averages to classical machine learning approaches. These baselines often show competitive performance compared to GNNs. In the present work, we take a step back and analyse the benchmark datasets via classical time series methods to uncover why spatially-unaware linear models pose a stronger competitor than previously reported, casting further doubt on the discriminative reliability of the aforementioned widely adopted datasets. Our statistical analysis provides a toolset for identifying significant spatial and temporal correlations, while revealing a structural bias introduced by first-order differenced datasets. We therefore recommend reducing the over-reliance on such datasets for method comparison, and instead advocate for more rigorous statistical evaluation. By applying the results of our analysis to a simple hybrid model, we show how our methodology can lead to novel ways of developing GNN models

</details>


### [186] [Jacobian-guided Noise Injection for Quantization Robustness in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20988)
*Deepanshu Pandey,Arnav Chavan,Nahush Lele,Sankalp Dayal,Deepak Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Jacobian-Guided Noise Injection的训练策略，通过基于注意力机制雅可比矩阵范数的高斯噪声注入，提升大语言模型量化时的稳定性。该方法有效缓解了软最大值运算对离散化误差的敏感性问题，在低比特量化设置下显著提升了模型性能，尤其在图像分类和语言建模任务中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自注意力机制对量化过程中的离散化误差极为敏感，尤其是软最大值运算容易受异常值和状态依赖的雅可比矩阵影响，导致性能下降。现有方法多依赖启发式或直接惩罚雅可比，缺乏理论指导和自适应能力。

Method: 提出基于雅可比矩阵弗罗贝尼乌斯范数计算噪声方差的零均值高斯噪声注入方法，动态调节噪声强度以匹配局部注意力敏感度，从而稳定量化过程。

Result: 在SOTA LLM架构上验证，该方法在低比特量化下相比主流PTQ方法显著提升鲁棒性；图像分类任务中，SigLIP在ImageNet-1K上的Top-1准确率相对提升最高达37%；语言模型在WikiText上相对困惑度降低最多达40%。

Conclusion: 所提出的Jacobian-Guided Noise Injection方法为量化敏感的自注意力机制提供了有效的理论支持与实践方案，证明了其在低比特量化场景下的优越性和普适性。

Abstract: Quantization of Large Language Models (LLMs) is often hindered by the sensitivity of the self-attention mechanism to discretization errors. We identify the softmax operator as a bottleneck for quantization stability due to its sensitivity to outliers and state-dependent Jacobian. We theoretically establish that suppressing the norm of this Jacobian helps in bounding quantization-induced performance degradation. Based on this, we propose Jacobian-Guided Noise Injection, a training strategy that injects zero-mean Gaussian noise into pre-attention logits, with variance derived directly from the Jacobian Frobenius norm. Unlike prior approaches that rely on heuristic or penalise jacobian directly, our method provides a way to identify the optimal noise variance based on the local attention sensitivity. We evaluate the method on SOTA LLM architectures, where it demonstrates improved robustness over popular PTQ methods. Empirical analysis reveals that the proposed method gives up to +37% relative gains on Top-1 accuracy on ImageNet-1K for SigLIP and improves relative perplexity by upto 40% on WikiText for language models in low bit quantisation settings, proving the efficacy of the approach.

</details>


### [187] [Trojaning the Alignment: Stealthy Backdoor Attacks against Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20991)
*Minhua Lin,Zhicheng Gao,Yilong Wang,Hanqing Lu,Xiang Zhang,Suhang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出STAG，一种针对文本属性图（TAGs）上图-语言对齐接口的隐蔽后门攻击框架。通过协同图触发生成器与文本侧软提示，使带有触发的图表示和触发后的文本表示共同指向目标类文本区域，同时利用候选检索和结构正则化实现触发节点的可读性及局部结构隐蔽性，有效应对标签图特有的隐蔽挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门攻击主要针对图或文本单模态，无法有效适应图-语言对齐场景；且在标签图中，触发模式暴露于文本和局部图结构中，易被检测或过滤，因此亟需一种能兼顾攻击有效性与隐蔽性的新型攻击方法。

Method: STAG通过联合设计图触发生成器与文本侧软提示，使图表示与文本表示在共享语义空间中协同偏移至目标类别区域；采用候选检索生成可读触发文本，并对触发子图进行结构正则化以保持其与原结构相似，从而实现隐蔽性。

Result: 在多个标签图数据集和代表性图基础模型上，STAG均表现出高攻击成功率和强隐蔽性，能够有效绕过常规检测机制。

Conclusion: STAG成功揭示了图-语言对齐模型在标签图上的后门脆弱性，为未来图基础模型的安全评估与防御提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) on text-attributed graphs (TAGs) align graph representations with language semantics to support transferable graph learning. Despite these advantages, the backdoor vulnerability of GFMs on TAGs remains insufficiently understood, especially under graph-language alignment, where graph and text representations are trained to constrain each other in a shared semantic space. Existing backdoor attacks mainly target either the graph side or the text side, treating the two modalities independently. This makes direct adaptation ineffective: graph-only triggers can be constrained by clean text semantics, while text-only triggers alter the language view but do not directly shift the graph representation being aligned and scored. TAGs also impose a stealth challenge because triggers are exposed as both node text and local graph structure, making incoherent trigger attributes or anomalous subgraphs easy to inspect or filter. In this paper, we propose STAG, a stealthy trojan attack framework designed for the graph-language alignment interface of GFMs on TAGs. STAG coordinates a graph-trigger generator with a text-side soft prompt so that trigger-attached graph representations and triggered text representations move toward the same target-class text region. To address TAG-specific stealthiness, STAG realizes trigger nodes as readable text through candidate retrieval and regularizes the trigger-attached subgraph so that its local structure remains close to the original subgraph. Extensive experiments on multiple TAG datasets and representative GFMs demonstrate the effectiveness and stealthiness of STAG. Our code is available at https://github.com/ventr1c/STAG.

</details>


### [188] [Free-Probability Kernels for Zero-Rollout Hyperparameter Selection in Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20998)
*Sara Malacarne,Andrea Ceni,Claudio Gallicchio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于自由概率理论的确定性方法，用于无需大量模拟即可选择漏失线性储层（leaky linear reservoirs）的超参数配置。通过分析跨滞后传播系数，构建大宽度极限下的确定性时间核，从而在短标记预演序列上进行核岭回归，快速评估不同工作模式的性能。该方法在十项合成时间基准测试中表现接近穷举模拟搜索（0.772 vs 0.774），同时节省了156,600次模拟；在真实电力变压器温度数据集和多变量蜂窝流量预测任务中也展现出优异的性能，显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统储备计算中的超参数（如递归增益、输入尺度、泄漏率）通常需通过大量滚动模拟来调优，效率低下。当验证模拟资源有限时，亟需一种高效、可推广的替代选择机制。

Method: 利用自由概率理论推导出跨滞后传播系数，建立大宽度极限下的确定性时间核，通过核岭回归对候选操作模式进行排序，实现零滚动或极小滚动预算下的超参数选择。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集上均表现出色：零滚动选择性能接近穷举搜索，且大幅减少计算开销；在低预算下优于随机搜索与贝叶斯优化，在多个任务中成功恢复最优配置。

Conclusion: 自由概率核可作为储备计算超参数选择的确定性代理模型，尤其适用于验证滚落稀缺的场景，显著提升效率并保持高性能。

Abstract: Reservoir computing (RC) couples a fixed recurrent dynamical system with a trained lightweight readout, but this efficiency is partly lost during hyperparameter selection: the recurrent gain, input scale, and leakage rate determine the reservoir's stability and temporal processing regime and are usually tuned through many rollouts. We introduce a deterministic, pilot-informed selector for leaky linear reservoirs followed by coordinate-wise nonlinear features. Free probability yields cross-lag propagation coefficients that summarize how the reservoir mixes past inputs. In the large-width limit, these coefficients define a deterministic temporal kernel that approximates the finite-reservoir feature geometry. Kernel ridge regression on a short labelled pilot sequence therefore ranks candidate operating regimes without instantiating or rolling out a reservoir, and the selected configuration transfers across widths. Across ten synthetic temporal benchmarks, zero-rollout selection obtains a mean deployment score of $0.772$, compared with $0.774$ for exhaustive simulation-based search, while avoiding $156\,600$ selection rollouts. With a small rollout budget, the proposed ranking provides the strongest mean performance at every tested budget and reaches the exhaustive reference using $4.8\%$ of its rollout cost. On four public electricity-transformer-temperature (ETT) forecasting datasets, five retained candidates recover the exhaustive operating point on three datasets. On multivariate cellular-traffic forecasting, 15 rollouts per cell reach the 462-rollout exhaustive reference and outperform random search and Bayesian optimization at low budgets. These results position free-probability kernels as deterministic surrogates for selecting reservoir operating regimes when validation rollouts are scarce.

</details>


### [189] [RODE: A Radial-Orthogonal Decoupled Engine for Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21024)
*Guoxiang Xu,Bince Qu,Qi Sun,Cheng Zhuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RODE提出一种新的矩阵优化方法，通过分离径向（范数）和方向更新，实现对神经网络权重更精确的控制。该方法在语言模型和图像分类任务中均优于现有优化器（如Muon），显著降低模型范数并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统矩阵感知优化器直接将条件化矩阵步长加到权重上，导致范数与方向耦合，影响学习过程；需解耦范数与方向以提升优化可控性与效率。

Method: RODE采用独立的径向更新规则（控制矩阵Frobenius范数）和方向更新规则（在切空间中进行Newton-Schulz条件化更新），实现范数与方向的分离优化。

Result: 在多个语言模型和图像分类任务中，RODE均超越Muon系列优化器，降低最终模型范数，并在1.5B和9B规模模型上取得更低损失与更高评估指标，尤其在GSM8K和MATH-500任务中表现最优。

Conclusion: 解耦径向与方向动态能提供更有效、更可控的矩阵优化路径，是现代神经网络训练中一种优越的优化策略。

Abstract: Modern neural network training increasingly uses matrix-aware optimizers, yet their conditioned matrix step is typically added directly to the weight, jointly changing its norm and direction. This interaction matters because the current norm determines angular motion, while directional learning can drive norm growth and thereby alter later steps. We introduce RODE, which gives the radial and directional components separate update rules and step sizes. RODE explicitly updates the matrix Frobenius norm through a scalar radial rule, while its directional channel performs Newton--Schulz-conditioned updates in the tangent space. Controlled GPT-2 interventions show gains from both direct norm control and RODE's directional update. Across two language-modeling and two image-classification tasks, RODE outperforms both Muon variants in every direct comparison and ends with lower full-model norms. At 1.5B scale, using the learning rate transferred directly from the Qwen2-style LM sweep, RODE lowers loss from 4.145 to 3.346 and final global norm from 11964 to 2183 relative to Muon RMS, with fixed-radius RODE improving further. For Qwen3.5-9B full-parameter fine-tuning, all six optimizers use the same tuning budget and the same formal-training and evaluation settings; RODE outperforms both Muon variants on all four evaluation tasks and attains the highest mean on GSM8K and MATH-500. Thus, decoupling radial and directional dynamics offers a more effective and controllable approach to matrix optimization.

</details>


### [190] [Designing a Robust LLM-Based Evaluation System for Agentic AI in Drug Discovery Through Human Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21057)
*Emma Granqvist,Rocío Mercado,Samuel Genheden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM作为评判者的评估框架，用于评估AstraZeneca部署的agentic药物发现助手ChatInvent。通过定义四个输出质量维度（完整性、相关性、结构清晰度、范围符合性）和工具调用正确性检查，验证了多个LLM作为评判者与人类专家的一致性，并通过少量示例优化最佳评判者，使其与人类多数意见的一致性从0.80提升至0.86。应用该框架于70个保留问题，发现非正式表达不会系统性降低输出质量，甚至有助于LLM在查询前重写原始问题。该框架可为科学领域中agentic系统的评估提供可复用模板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法如BLEU、ROUGE无法捕捉语义正确性，而人工评估难以满足agentic LLM系统的快速迭代需求。尽管LLM-as-a-Judge成为可扩展替代方案，但缺乏对评判者与人类专家一致性验证，限制其可信度。因此亟需一种可验证且可复用的评估框架以支持科学领域中agentic系统的真实评估。

Method: 构建包含四个输出质量维度（完整性、相关性、结构清晰度、范围符合性）及工具调用正确性的评估框架；通过五名专家标注进行人类对齐研究，对比Gemini 3.1 Pro、Claude Opus 4.7、GPT-5、Llama 3.1 70B等模型作为评判者的性能；使用少量人类标注示例进行少样本提示优化，提升最优模型与人类共识的一致性；最后在70个保留问题上应用优化后的评判者，分析系统局限性。

Result: 优化后的LLM评判者与人类多数意见的一致性由0.80提升至0.86；发现非正式表达不降低输出质量，甚至有利于LLM重写问题后提升效果；揭示了当前agentic系统在自然语言处理与任务理解上的潜在瓶颈。

Conclusion: 所提出的评估框架实现了人类对齐的可扩展评估，为科学领域中agentic LLM系统的开发与部署提供了可靠、可复用的评估标准，推动了自动化药物发现流程的可信演进。

Abstract: Agentic large language model (LLM) systems are reshaping scientific workflows in chemistry and drug discovery, but evaluating their open-ended, tool-augmented outputs remains a fundamental bottleneck. Reference-based metrics such as BLEU and ROUGE fail to capture semantic correctness, while expert human evaluation does not scale to the iteration speed these systems demand. The LLM-as-a-Judge paradigm has emerged as a scalable alternative, but existing drug discovery benchmarks deploy LLM judges without validating their alignment with human experts. In this work, we present an LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation framework for ChatInvent, an agentic drug discovery assistant deployed at AstraZeneca, with four contributions. First, we define four output-quality evaluation dimensions---Completeness, Relevancy, Structural Clarity, and Scope Adherence---alongside deterministic Tool Call Correctness checks. Second, we validate the judge through a human alignment study with five expert annotators, comparing Gemini 3.1 Pro, Claude Opus 4.7, GPT-5, and Llama 3.1 70B as candidate judges. Third, we optimize the best-performing judge using few-shot demonstrations of human-annotated examples, improving alignment with the human majority vote from 0.80 to 0.86. Fourth, applying the optimized judge to 70 held-out questions, we surface concrete limitations and find that informal phrasings do not systematically degrade output quality; if anything, it is helpful to have the LLM rewrite the original question before querying the agent. Our framework provides a reusable template for human-aligned evaluation of agentic systems in scientific domains.

</details>


### [191] [TracingFlow: A Simulation-Free Trajectory Inference Framework Based on Second-Order Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21070)
*Yuhao Sun,Zekun Wu,Zixun Huang,Peijie Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TracingFlow 是一种无需模拟的流匹配框架，可推广至二阶动力学，通过神经网络回归加速度场，精确高效地解决动态最优加速度传输（DOAT）问题。相比一阶方法生成的过于平滑轨迹，其二阶公式能捕捉高曲率转变和非线性演化，学习潜在力场，在复杂合成数据和大规模单细胞RNA测序数据上表现出更优的分布重建与轨迹保真度。结合谱系追踪先验，可恢复数学最优且生物合理的动态结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于最优传输的方法多局限于一阶动力学，假设无记忆的速度场，无法刻画细胞分化等过程中存在的调控动量和时滞响应等复杂动态特性，限制了模型表达能力。

Method: 提出 TracingFlow 框架，采用神经网络直接回归加速度场，实现对二阶动力学系统的建模，求解 Dynamical Optimal Acceleration Transport (DOAT) 问题，无需仿真即可生成精准轨迹。

Result: 在合成数据和真实scRNA-seq数据上均优于现有方法，在分布重建和轨迹保真度方面表现更佳；结合谱系信息可恢复符合生物学意义的动态结构。

Conclusion: TracingFlow 通过二阶动力学建模，显著提升了对复杂系统演化过程的推断能力，为单细胞时空动态分析提供了高效、准确且生物合理的解决方案。

Abstract: Inferring continuous system evolution from sparse temporal snapshots is a key challenge in generative modeling and single-cell omics. While Optimal Transport (OT) is popular, existing frameworks are largely restricted to first-order dynamics, assuming memoryless velocity fields. This limits expressiveness, as first-order systems fail to account for regulatory momentum and time-delayed responses inherent in processes like cell differentiation. Here, we introduce TracingFlow, a simulation-free Flow Matching framework generalizing to second-order dynamics. By using neural networks to regress the acceleration field, TracingFlow provides an exact, efficient solution to the Dynamical Optimal Acceleration Transport (DOAT) problem. Unlike first-order methods yielding over-smoothed trajectories, our second-order formulation captures high-curvature transitions and nonlinear evolutions by learning the underlying force fields. Evaluated on complex synthetic and large-scale scRNA-seq datasets, TracingFlow achieves superior accuracy in distributional reconstruction and trajectory faithfulness. Moreover, by integrating lineage tracing priors, it recovers dynamical structures that are both mathematically optimal and biologically plausible.

</details>


### [192] [Causal Modeling of Adverse Pregnancy Outcomes via Adaptive LLM Proposals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21079)
*Kavimayil P. Komarasamy,Saurabh Mathur,Ameet Soni,David M. Haas,Kristian Kersting,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号框架，结合大语言模型（LLM）的先验知识与数据实证评分，迭代生成可解释的因果假设，用于探索不良妊娠结局（APOs）的成因。该方法将LLM视为自适应提案分布，生成候选因果图并基于真实临床数据进行评分，再利用高分结果更新LLM上下文，引导后续生成向更优区域收敛。在真实临床数据上的实验表明，该方法不仅恢复了专家构建图中的所有有效边，还发现了专家未列出的新潜在因果关系，为干预策略提供了新线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不良妊娠结局（如早产、妊娠期糖尿病）对母婴健康有长期影响，但其成因尚不明确。现有数据驱动方法受限于数据稀疏和领域知识不完整，而大语言模型输出又存在不一致或矛盾问题，亟需一种融合先验知识与实证数据的可靠因果发现方法。

Method: 提出一种神经符号框架，利用大语言模型生成因果假设，将其作为自适应提案分布；通过实证数据对生成的因果图进行评分；将高分图用于更新LLM的上下文，引导下一轮生成向更优假设空间聚焦，实现迭代优化。

Result: 在真实临床数据集上评估，该方法成功恢复了专家构建因果图中的全部有效边，并识别出多个专家未列出的合理因果关系，揭示了潜在的新型干预路径。

Conclusion: 所提神经符号框架有效整合了大语言模型的广泛知识与数据实证能力，克服了纯数据驱动方法的局限性，在复杂医学因果发现任务中展现出优越性能，具备推动临床研究与精准干预的潜力。

Abstract: Adverse Pregnancy Outcomes (APOs) such as preterm birth and gestational diabetes can have long-term consequences for both the mother and child, yet an understanding of their causes remains elusive. Causal discovery in this domain is especially challenging due to a paucity of data and incomplete domain knowledge. As a result, pure data-driven methods fail, and Large Language Model (LLM) outputs remain inconsistent or contradictory. We introduce a neurosymbolic framework for generating plausible causal hypotheses that iteratively combines the broad prior knowledge of LLMs with empirical scoring on data. Our method treats the LLM as an adaptive proposal distribution, generating hypotheses that are scored against empirical data; the resulting high-scoring graphs are then used to update the LLM's context, steering subsequent generations toward more promising regions of the hypothesis space. We evaluate our approach on a real-world clinical dataset for modeling APOs and their risk factors, comparing our results against an expert-constructed causal graph. Our method recovers all expert-validated edges and identifies additional plausible causal relations not previously listed by experts, potentially providing new insights for targeted interventions.

</details>


### [193] [FlatLand: Personalized Graph Federated Learning via Tailored Lorentz Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21096)
*Jiahong Liu,Ram Samarth B B,Xinyu Fu,Menglin Yang,Weixi Zhang,Rex Ying,Irwin King*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlatLand 是一种新颖的个性化联邦学习方法，通过将不同客户端的数据嵌入到超球几何的洛伦兹空间中，利用超球几何对真实世界图结构固有的负曲率特性进行自然建模。该方法通过时间类参数编码客户端特异性异质性，空间类参数保留共性知识，实现参数解耦，从而无需估计客户端相似性或额外计算模块即可直接聚合。在多种联邦图学习任务中，FlatLand 在低维设置下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化联邦学习方法忽略了图结构内在的几何特性，尤其是在客户端数据具有高度异质性的图联邦学习场景中，难以有效建模复杂结构差异。因此需要一种能捕捉图结构内在负曲率并有效处理客户端异质性的新方法。

Method: 提出 FlatLand 方法，将客户端数据嵌入到超球几何的洛伦兹空间中；设计参数解耦策略，将异质信息（时间类参数）与共性知识（空间类参数）分离，支持直接聚合，避免依赖客户端相似性估计和额外模块。

Result: 在多个联邦图学习任务上，FlatLand 显著优于现有方法，尤其在低维设置下表现突出，验证了其在建模异质图结构和提升性能方面的有效性。

Conclusion: FlatLand 通过引入超球几何中的洛伦兹空间，有效建模了图结构的内在负曲率和客户端异质性，实现了高效且精准的个性化联邦学习，为复杂异构图数据的协作训练提供了新范式。

Abstract: Federated learning enables privacy-preserving collaborative training, but highly heterogeneous client data remain challenging, especially in graph federated learning where clients possess structurally diverse graphs. Existing personalized federated learning (PFL) methods ignore the intrinsic geometric properties of diverse graph structures. We propose FlatLand, a novel personalized federated learning method that embeds different clients' data in tailored Lorentz space of hyperbolic geometry. Our key insight is that hyperbolic geometry naturally accommodates the intrinsic negative curvature prevalent in real-world graphs, while the time-like dimension in Lorentz space provides a principled way to encode client-specific heterogeneity. We develop a parameter decoupling strategy that separates heterogeneous information (captured in time-like parameters) from common knowledge (preserved in space-like parameters), enabling direct aggregation without requiring client similarity estimation and extra calculation modules. Empirical results on diverse federated graph learning tasks demonstrate that FlatLand achieves superior performance, particularly in low-dimensional settings.

</details>


### [194] [BackDFL: A Unified Benchmark For Backdoor Attacks and Defenses In Decentralized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21137)
*Mouhamed Amine Bouchiha,Gregory Blanc,Yufei Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BackDFL提出一个统一的基准，系统评估去中心化联邦学习（DFL）在现实且自适应的后门攻击下的表现。研究发现，现有最先进的抗拜占庭攻击的DFL方法和防御策略在仅15%恶意参与率下即失效，尤其在异构设置中，其鲁棒性随通信拓扑结构变化显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对去中心化联邦学习（DFL）安全性的研究依赖于简化的威胁模型、非自适应攻击者、不一致的评估协议和训练配置，导致对其鲁棒性的理解不全面。因此需要一个更真实、系统的评估框架来揭示DFL在实际攻击下的脆弱性。

Method: 提出BackDFL，一个统一的基准测试平台，用于在多种通信拓扑、不同恶意参与率和异构数据分布条件下，系统评估DFL在自适应后门攻击下的表现。

Result: 实验表明，即使在低比例恶意节点（如15%）的情况下，主流的抗拜占庭和防后门方法也普遍失效，尤其是在异构数据和特定通信图结构下，鲁棒性差异明显。

Conclusion: DFL的安全性被严重高估；现有防御机制在现实攻击场景下表现不佳，亟需重新设计更具鲁棒性的去中心化学习框架。

Abstract: Decentralized Federated Learning (DFL) promises trust-free collaborative learning by replacing the centralized parameter server with peer-to-peer model exchange. However, this architectural shift fundamentally reshapes the threat landscape. Without globally coordinated aggregation, DFL becomes particularly susceptible to backdoor attacks, in which malicious participants implant persistent hidden behaviors while maintaining high clean-task performance. In this paper, we argue that the robustness of DFL has been significantly overestimated. Existing studies rely on simplified threat models, non-adaptive adversaries, fragmented evaluation protocols, inconsistent communication topologies, and ad hoc training configurations, leading to an incomplete understanding of DFL security. To address these limitations, we present BackDFL, a unified benchmark for systematically evaluating DFL under realistic and adaptive backdoor attacks. Through extensive experiments, BackDFL exposes critical failure modes of decentralized learning. Our results demonstrate that both state-of-the-art Byzantine-robust DFL methods and adapted FL backdoor defenses fail under modest malicious participation rates (as low as 15%), especially in heterogeneous settings, while their robustness varies substantially across communication graph topologies.

</details>


### [195] [COEC: Calibrated Orthogonal-Equivalence Compensation for Structured Pruning of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21142)
*Peiqi Yu,Nam Ling,Wei Wang,Wei Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COEC是一种无需训练的补偿框架，通过交替应用左右正交旋转和奇异值重缩放，提升结构化剪枝后大语言模型的性能。它利用小校准集的二阶统计信息，在不进行反向传播或参数重训练的情况下，有效缓解剪枝带来的精度下降。该方法在多个模型家族（如Llama-3、Qwen2.5）和不同稀疏度下均表现出优于现有方法的零样本准确率与困惑度表现，尤其在高稀疏度时提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练-free补偿方法仅使用加性偏置或单个正交旋转，无法改变输入的奇异方向，限制了剪枝后权重的自适应能力。为更充分地利用保留权重的表达潜力，需引入更灵活的几何变换机制。

Method: 提出COEC框架，采用交替左右正交旋转，并在约化Stiefel流形上优化右旋转；通过广义交叉验证确定各层正则化强度以调整奇异值；引入校准格拉姆矩阵的调制和注意力投影间的对齐惩罚项，保持相邻层间几何关系。所有组件基于小校准集的二阶统计量构建，无需反向传播或重训练。

Result: 在Llama-3、Llama-3.1和Qwen2.5系列模型上，多种结构化稀疏度下，COEC均提升了困惑度和多数情况下的零样本准确率，且在高稀疏度下增益更大，表明其能有效恢复因列剪枝损失的性能。

Conclusion: COEC通过更灵活的几何补偿机制，显著提升了结构化剪枝后大语言模型的性能，证明了后剪枝补偿的有效性，且具备通用性与高效性，适用于多种剪枝策略。

Abstract: Structured pruning reduces the size and inference cost of large language models (LLMs) by removing weight columns, but the resulting output error can degrade accuracy. Existing training-free compensation methods use an additive bias or a single orthogonal rotation on the output side of the retained weight. These corrections leave its input singular frame unchanged and therefore limit how the retained weight can adapt after column removal. We propose COEC (Calibrated Orthogonal-Equivalence Compensation), a training-free compensation framework that applies alternating left and right orthogonal rotations to the retained weight. The right rotation is optimized on a reduced Stiefel manifold, while singular values are rescaled using generalized cross-validation to select the regularization strength for each layer. COEC further tempers the calibration Gram matrix to reduce the dominance of high-energy activation directions and introduces an alignment penalty that preserves the geometric relation between adjacent attention projections.All components use second-order statistics from a small calibration set and require neither backpropagation through the LLM nor retraining of the model parameters. COEC is independent of the column pruning criterion and can be applied to multiple structured pruning methods. Experiments on the Llama-3, Llama-3.1, and Qwen2.5 model families across multiple structured sparsity levels show that COEC improves perplexity on every model and zero-shot accuracy in most settings over existing compensation methods, with larger gains at higher sparsity. These results show that post-pruning compensation can recover part of the performance lost to column removal.

</details>


### [196] [Capturing Cardiac Cyclicity through Phase-Equivariant Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21147)
*Blaise Delaney,Dominic Dootson,Juan Jose Juan Castella,Salil Patel,Andrew Pfaff,Yuji Xing,Jonny Hancox,Karin Sevegnani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Winder提出了一种基于心脏周期相位等变性的自监督学习方法，通过固定且解析的传输算子构建相位不变坐标与相位旋转的谐波子空间，在仅约100万参数下实现了与先进自监督方法相当的诊断准确率，并保持了可解释的潜在几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用生理过程的循环结构作为自监督表示学习的先验知识，特别是心脏周期提供了明确的周期性框架，以更好地建模和保留诊断有用信息。

Method: 设计了一种相位等变的自监督目标函数，引入Winder联合嵌入架构，将表示组织为相位不变坐标和相位旋转的谐波子空间；其传输算子基于周期几何特性解析推导，无需学习且不增加参数。

Result: 在PTB-XL数据集上，使用冻结线性探测协议评估，Winder在约100万参数规模下达到与当前先进自监督方法相当的诊断性能，同时展现出相位等变的潜在几何特性。

Conclusion: 显式编码心脏相位对称性可在保留诊断信息的同时，实现参数高效、可解释且与可测量生理量直接关联的潜在表示结构。

Abstract: The cyclic structure of physiological processes offers a natural prior for self-supervised representation learning, and the cardiac cycle provides a particularly well-defined setting in which to exploit it. We derive a phase-equivariant self-supervised objective and introduce Winder, a joint-embedding architecture that organises representations into phase-invariant coordinates and phase-rotating harmonic subspaces. Its transport operator is fixed and closed-form, derived from the cycle's geometry rather than learned, and adds no parameters. Evaluated on PTB-XL under a frozen linear-probe protocol, Winder attains diagnostic accuracy within the range reported by state-of-the-art self-supervised methods at a ~1 M parameter footprint, while exhibiting phase-equivariant latent geometry. These findings demonstrate that explicitly encoding cardiac-phase symmetry can preserve diagnostically useful information while yielding a latent geometry that is legible, parameter-efficient, and directly tied to a measurable physiological quantity.

</details>


### [197] [Thermo-FL: Thermal-Aware Robust Federated Fine-Tuning of Large Language Models for Edge AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21172)
*Shiva Shrestha,Kazi Shaharair Sharif,Zongxing Xie,Jiajing Huang,Anhao Xiang,Honghui Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Thermo-FL is a thermal-aware federated LoRA fine-tuning framework that addresses hardware instability and adversarial update corruption in edge device deployments. It dynamically adjusts local training and update transmission based on device temperature, reducing workload under thermal stress. On the server side, TERRA, a robust aggregation pipeline, handles sparse updates using norm filtering, mask-aware validation, adaptive clipping, and mask-aware aggregation. Evaluated via emulator and physical Jetson testbed, Thermo-FL improves robustness against attacks, stabilizes temperature, reduces upload size via bitmap encoding, and maintains model utility under various perturbations.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Practical deployment of federated fine-tuning for large language models on edge devices faces challenges from thermal constraints (e.g., throttling, slow training) and adversarial threats (e.g., Byzantine clients, communication-layer attacks). Existing methods do not jointly address hardware dynamics and security vulnerabilities.

Method: Thermo-FL uses device temperature as an active control signal to adjust the fraction of active LoRA layers and update density on clients. On the server, TERRA performs robust aggregation of sparse LoRA updates through multiple mechanisms: norm filtering, mask-aware directional validation, adaptive active-coordinate clipping, and mask-aware aggregation.

Result: In simulations, Thermo-FL achieves top BoolQ accuracy across clean and attack scenarios while remaining competitive on GSM8K. In physical tests with Jetson devices, it stabilizes temperature, reduces compressed upload size via bitmap sparse encoding, and preserves GSM8K performance under sign-flip/scale and MITM attacks.

Conclusion: Secure edge adaptation of large language models requires integrated consideration of hardware behavior, workload regulation, sparse communication, and robust aggregation. Thermo-FL demonstrates that thermal awareness and robust sparse aggregation significantly enhance reliability and security in real-world federated fine-tuning.

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning enables large language models to adapt on edge devices without centralizing private data, but practical deployments must address hardware instability and adversarial update corruption together. Thermally constrained clients may throttle, slow local training, or delay synchronous aggregation, while Byzantine clients and communication-layer adversaries can corrupt the updates used to form the global model. To address these challenges, we present Thermo-FL, a thermal-aware federated LoRA fine-tuning framework that uses device temperature as an active control signal for local adapter training and sparse update transmission. On the client side, Thermo-FL adjusts the active LoRA-layer fraction and transmitted update density as devices heat or cool, reducing workload under thermal stress. On the server side, Thermo-FL introduces TERRA, a robust aggregation pipeline for dynamically sparse LoRA updates that combines norm filtering, mask-aware directional validation, adaptive active-coordinate clipping, and mask-aware aggregation. We evaluate Thermo-FL using both a large-scale emulator and a Jetson-based physical testbed. In the emulator, Thermo-FL improves robustness under adversarial sparse aggregation and achieves the strongest BoolQ accuracy across clean and attack settings while remaining competitive on GSM8K. In the physical prototype, Thermo-FL stabilizes device temperature, reduces compressed upload size through bitmap sparse encoding, and preserves GSM8K utility under sign-flip/scale and MITM perturbations. These results show that secure edge LLM adaptation should jointly consider hardware behavior, workload regulation, sparse communication, and aggregation robustness.

</details>


### [198] [A Neurosymbolic Approach for Constructing Planning Domain Models from Clinical Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21186)
*Ranveer Singh,Saurabh Mathur,Michael Skinner,Prasad Tadepalli,Kristian Kersting,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NSPIN，一种从非结构化临床叙述中推导概率规划领域模型的神经符号框架。该方法利用预训练语言模型提取并补全结构化事件序列，进而生成PPDDL模型，并通过语言模型提出的修订建议在实证验证指导下优化其前提条件。在9名外科医生撰写的2,660份腹腔镜阑尾切除术病历上评估，结果表明所生成模型具备对未见病历的泛化能力，且专家评审认为其知识与临床实践高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腹腔镜阑尾切除等手术流程复杂且高风险，但其工作流程的形式化以支持决策仍面临挑战。现有方法如经验性符号方法和大语言模型（LLMs）难以有效处理临床文本中的非结构化事件数据及隐含操作，导致无法充分建模手术过程。因此需要一种能结合语言理解与逻辑推理的新方法来实现更准确的手术流程建模。

Method: NSPIN框架首先利用预训练语言模型从原始临床文本中提取和补全结构化事件序列；随后基于这些序列构建概率规划描述语言（PPDDL）模型；最后通过大语言模型提出可能的条件修正，并在实证数据验证基础上进行迭代优化，从而提升模型准确性与临床一致性。

Result: 在2,660份腹腔镜阑尾切除术病历上的实验表明，NSPIN生成的模型能够有效泛化至未见过的病例；经临床专家评审，模型所体现的知识与实际外科操作基本一致，证明了其有效性与实用性。

Conclusion: NSPIN成功实现了从非结构化临床文本中自动推导出符合实际手术流程的概率规划模型，克服了传统方法在处理隐含动作和缺乏结构化数据方面的局限，为手术流程形式化与智能决策支持提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Surgical procedures such as laparoscopic appendectomy are complex, high-stakes processes, yet formalizing their workflows for decision support remains a significant challenge. Inducing probabilistic planning domain models in this setting is particularly difficult due to the lack of structured event data and the prevalence of implicit actions in clinical narratives, which neither empirical symbolic methods nor Large Language Models (LLMs) can adequately address on their own. We introduce NSPIN, a neurosymbolic framework for inducing probabilistic planning domain models from unstructured clinical narratives. Our method extracts and imputes structured event sequences from raw text using a pretrained LLM, then induces a PPDDL model and refines its preconditions with LLM-proposed revisions, guided by empirical validation. We evaluate the approach on 2,660 laparoscopic appendectomy notes written by 9 surgeons. NSPIN yields models that generalize to unseen notes, and expert clinical review indicates its induced knowledge is largely consistent with surgical practice.

</details>


### [199] [Tydra: An Efficient Hybrid Model for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21199)
*Mieszko Komisarczyk,Saurabh Mathur,Maurice Kraus,Sriraam Natarajan,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tydra 是一种混合 Transformer-状态空间模型 (SSM) 架构，通过交错注意力和 SSM 层，在保持高预测性能的同时显著降低推理时间。在 30 个 OpenML 数据集上，其推理速度比 TabPFN 快 30%，且优于一个约大十倍的 Hydra 模型，表明混合架构是表格式基础模型的有前景方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Transformer-based 模型（如 TabPFN）虽性能强但计算成本为二次方，而 SSM-based 模型（如 Hydra）虽高效但牺牲了准确性。需要一种既能保持高性能又能提升效率的平衡方案。

Method: 提出 Tydra，一种将注意力层与 SSM 层交错排列的混合架构，用于表格式上下文学习，以兼顾性能与效率。

Result: 在 30 个 OpenML 数据集上，Tydra 推理时间比 TabPFN 减少 30%，同时保持接近其性能；且优于约十倍大的 Hydra 模型，兼具更快推理与更高准确率。

Conclusion: 混合架构（Transformer-SSM）是构建高效且高性能表格式基础模型的可行且有前景的方向。

Abstract: Transformer-based tabular foundation models such as TabPFN achieve strong predictive performance but incur quadratic computational cost with context length. On the other hand, subquadratic SSM-based alternatives such as Hydra trade away accuracy for efficiency. To balance both, we introduce Tydra, a hybrid Transformer-State Space Model (SSM) architecture for tabular in-context learning that interleaves attention and SSM layers. Across 30 OpenML datasets, Tydra reduces inference time by 30% relative to TabPFN while retaining much of its predictive performance. Tydra also outperforms an approximately ten-times-larger Hydra model while providing faster inference. The results indicate that hybrid architectures are a promising direction for tabular foundation models.

</details>


### [200] [Curriculum-Aware Interpolate-then-Refine: Learned Physiological Time-Series Imputation under Realistic Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21207)
*Yu-Chao Huang,Haochen Zhang,Nicholas Konz,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAIR是一种针对生理时间序列（如血压、血糖）缺失数据的两阶段插补框架，通过先粗略拟合基线曲线再逐步修正以提升生理合理性，结合双向GRU插补器与Transformer修正器，在多种缺失机制下均优于现有方法，尤其在复杂缺失场景下表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用插补方法在真实临床生理信号中表现不佳，因忽略两个关键特性：缺失可能发生在信号极端而非典型状态，且缺失长度跨度极大。因此需要一种能适应这些特性的新方法。

Method: 提出基于课程学习的‘先插补后修正’（CAIR）框架，包含双向GRU插补模块和三轮迭代的Transformer修正模块，联合训练于广泛随机缺失的课程策略下，逐步优化插补结果的生理合理性。

Result: 在连续血糖监测（AI-READI）和重症监护血压数据（MIMIC-III）上，CAIR在所有缺失机制（MCAR、MAR、NMAR）下均表现最优；在价值相关缺失（NMAR）下相比最强基线提升达19%；同时在重建误差与临床可操作指标之间取得良好平衡。

Conclusion: CAIR通过分阶段、迭代式修正策略有效应对生理信号缺失的复杂性，显著优于现有方法，是临床时间序列插补的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Imputing physiological time series (arterial blood pressure, blood glucose, etc.) is essential for addressing the missingness that pervades clinical data. Yet modern imputation methods perform poorly in this domain: a recent benchmark found that simple linear interpolation outperformed every learned imputer on real-world clinical signals with realistic gaps. We show that this reflects two properties of physiological missingness that generic imputers ignore: gaps may occur when the signal is clinically extreme rather than typical, and gap lengths can easily span orders of magnitude. To this end, we introduce Curriculum-Aware Interpolate-then-Refine (CAIR), a two-stage framework for physiological time-series imputation. Our key motivation is to learn a coarse base curve and then repeatedly correct it toward physiological realism, rather than predict a gap in a single pass. Consequently, CAIR couples a bidirectional-GRU interpolator with a Transformer refiner that corrects its own estimate over three successive passes, trained jointly under a broad, signal-agnostic random-gap curriculum. We evaluate imputers stratified by gap length and missingness mechanism (MCAR, MAR, NMAR) rather than by a single average, and CAIR is the most accurate under every mechanism on continuous glucose monitoring (AI-READI) and arterial pressure in intensive care (MIMIC-III). Its margin over the strongest baseline grows with difficulty, from 9% under MCAR to 19% under value-dependent dropout, where generic learned imputers are weakest. We further show low reconstruction error alone does not recover the burden metrics clinicians act on: interpolants matching CAIR's error fail to preserve those metrics, imputers that recover them are far less accurate, and CAIR alone ranks among the best on both axes.

</details>


### [201] [TRACE-C: Rank-Calibrated Relational Anomaly Detection for Multi-Stream Operational Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21251)
*Matthew Faucher*

Main category: cs.LG
